tbmm-2019-12-05sit01spe0022par0117	Thank you, Mr. President. The property of Dulkadir Beyi Alauddevle Bozkurt Bey in Osmaniye Kadirli district was built as a Roman basilica between 425 and 450 years after the foundation of Ala Cami, founded in the name of Bozkurt Bey, during the Byzantine period. From 1480 to 1490, the historical structure was used as a mosque until 1924. It was abandoned in ruins after 1924. In order to restore this important cultural presence, the Foundation's Administration established restoration in 2014, in accordance with the Regional Council for Cultural Preservation approved projects. A polycarbon-based cover has been created over the decision of the Protection Council, considering that the mosaics and building elements released during the restoration are not affected by the sun, rain and natural conditions, but also by the opinions of expert scientists on the need for the structure to breathe. As alleged, the veil in the structure is not intended to complete the missing elements of the structure, but to protect it. With the project's recent completion, Ala Cami, which has been closed for nearly a century, will be re-opened, and the trails of Roman, Byzantine, fiefs and Ottoman times will be visible. I salute the General Assembly.

tbmm-2019-12-05sit04spe0270par0019	Mr. President, I would like to express my opinion on Article 59, which has changed the Act of Associations 5253. The proposal for amendments on Article 59 (P.S.), the board of directors and the board of audits and the original and reserve members of the association have to report the name, last name, birth date and identity number to the management of property within 30 days. The proposal calls for the absence of problems such as membership in associations without the knowledge of citizens and the absence of procedures of those who have withdrawn from membership. This law does not justify the change intended to be brought into Article 59, because there are sufficient sanctions against the grounds set in the Turkish Penal Code. It seems that the goal is to prevent organization from being thrown out of fear. If you block people's organization directly or indirectly, you will open the way for illegal organisation, which is what you use a lot of. Freedom of organization can be described as freedom to come together by creating a collective organization that represents themselves in order to protect the interests of individuals. The freedom to form an organization is primarily within the right to form an organisation and become a member of the organisation. This freedom is also a freedom of choice and includes the right to not be a member of an organisation. Freedom of organization has been protected in Article 20 of the Universal Declaration of Human Rights, Article 22 of the International Convention on Civil and Political Rights, which Turkey is a party to, and Article 11 of the European Court of Human Rights. Turkey has ratified and passed all of these documents in accordance with the procedure of its freedom of organisation, which have become part of internal law in accordance with Article 90 of the Constitution. According to Article 11 of the European Convention on Human Rights, and Article 33, 51, 53, 54, 68 and 69, the subject of the freedom of the Constitution is everyone. You can't stop this organization directly or indirectly. In this regard, the European Court of Human Rights ruled against Bulgaria's state in its 2007 ruling that it was not right to prevent an organisation from being effectively used by the state by difficult conditions to overcome and thus effectively use its freedom of organisation. Freedom of organization is closely associated with democracy, and its importance in this context has been adopted by the Constitutional Court. According to the court, the existence of organisations in democracies where citizens can come together and follow common goals is an important component of a healthy society. In democracies, such an organization has basic rights to be respected and protected by the state. When there is a legal discrepancy with freedom of organisation, these issues should be in the standards set up by international contracts, or especially by the European Court of Human Rights, the Human Rights Committee. If you, the European Court of Human Rights, which I consider to be above, insist that this clause be passed by ignoring the Constitutional Court's rulings, you will put the state on the line internationally and pay serious compensations. The country is not ruled by fear and fear. Congressmans, remember that democracy is not freedom to destroy freedom. The way a country develops is not to silence different ideas, but to prevent organization of people who don't think like you; rather, to listen respectfully to other people's ideas, to open the way for them to be organized, to be convinced, or to convince them. Democracy is the regime of reconciliation and balance to ensure social peace, I salute you all.

Dear lawmakers, any bill brought to parliament is presented as a bag of legislation. Now, I'm going to tell you about the first time in the history of law that bag law came out and its adventure when it came out. The first bag of legislation was introduced in Rome. In Rome, where traditional law was not previously written, it was asked by the people in 452 B.C. to be elected as a council of 10 and to make traditional law written. The council worked for two years, writing down traditional law in all areas of the law, and writing it on large plates, and hanging it on the biggest square in Rome. Sixty years later, the invasion of Rome and the destruction of these plates began to create turmoil in the field of law, and the only law that was implemented in Turkey today began to produce laws that contained different subjects. In particular, a matter which may be considered a bribe to the inside of the law in order for the law to be passed in order that the public may not approve, which may cause its reaction, was thus easily passed through the House. For example, a tax law that would react in society was included in the issue of giving Roman citizenship to all those living on Italian soil at the time, and the community's response was attempted to avoid. This continued until 98 B.C.E., but after a law passed this year, objections were raised and a principle that banned the bag was accepted. Article 2 of the law passed at that time was firmly established in the rule that it is not right for different issues to be collected by one law, but that they should be passed by separate laws. Dear lawmakers, in today's advanced democracies, this principle is followed, and each issue is brought to parliament with a separate proposal. In our country, especially after the coup of September 12th, law enforcement and bag laws have begun intensively. In the new Turkey, rather than decrees on the rule of law, presidential decrees have been routinely adopted and the Grand National Assembly of Turkey has been disabled. In recent years, in particular, the legal logic of the bag of legislation applied by the AK PARTY governments has, unfortunately, been bankrupted, made into puzzle laws. Dear friends, in the current case of Turkish Civil Law, any adult who poses a danger to society by one of the causes of mental illness, mental weakness, alcohol or drug addiction, severe danger of infectious disease or vagrancy may be placed in an institution suitable for treatment, education or rehabilitation. As they perform their duties, public officials who have learned of the existence of one of these reasons must immediately report the situation to the competent guardianship office. The person involved is removed from the institution immediately. It is still the main law for serious mental disorders or involuntary treatments in alcohol or drug abuse that are a danger to society in our country. With the 55th article that we bring, we add the 6th and 7th jokes after the current 5th joke. It also suggests that in order to ensure that the official health board report can be obtained in the context of the limitation of freedom for protection, various samples can be taken from a person's body, necessary medical treatments can be performed, can be placed in a health care institution for 20 days, if necessary, and can be used to enforce decisions made under this clause. As it turns out, these arrangements include interfering with a person's body integrity without consent or force, while the freedom of one's interest can be restricted for 20 days on a simple doctor's preliminary report. The immunity of the person's body integrity has been secured by the 17th article of our Constitution, and the 3rd and 8th Articles of the European Convention on Human Rights. The immunity of body integrity is one of the most fundamental values of our legal system. The idea of ensuring that the official health board report can be received under any circumstances is not a legitimate goal to justify interfering with a person's body integrity by force. On the other hand, the regulation of a person's freedom to be restricted for up to 20 days with a simple doctor's preliminary report, which is completely open to joy, which offers no protection to the individual, is also contrary to Article 5 of the European Convention on Human Rights. In fact, dear friends, if such legal regulations are carried out in such a manner, the facts will be admitted to health care institutions in an involuntary and involuntary manner before the official health board report, which will be based on the restrictions of their freedoms for treatment in the court. In this way, the process of hospitalization will be complicated for the purpose of treatment with the expert services, preventing involuntary hospitalization and medical treatments. Such an application is not primarily acceptable in terms of patient rights and human rights. I want to underline that in particular. On the other hand, the demand for the preliminary report to be organised in applications under intensive service of today's psychiatric clinics will lead to frequent deposits and unnecessary observation decisions. In this regard, the legal arrangement will provide an additional load of patients, including administrative, legal and so-called problems in closed psychiatric clinics, which already have a limited number of psychiatric beds, if judges decide without delay. Dear friends, especially our members of the Commission, I don't understand why you would put a health-related substance into such an unrelated proposal out of the blue, and as a doctor, I honestly don't. Besides, here, you're legalizing blood, nails, and hair samples from a person who's mentally unstable and unconscious. We're making the situation more legitimate with the legal arrangement that enhances acts of violence. When the law comes out like this, you put these people and the paramedics and law enforcement in a more stressful environment, and you lay the groundwork for paintings that can lead to bad events. The government will be an instrument of violence; I say let's stop it. There's a lot of violence going on in our country, folks. If this law is passed like this, because we've provided more environments, the violence in health will increase day by day, and I think the plague will be severe.
